*Natchanon Pollertlam,Witchayut Kornsuwannawit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对长对话场景中三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测记忆）的服务成本进行了基准测试，与两种参考策略（固定滚动窗口和重发完整转录）对比，发现记忆系统的成本无法由对话长度和信息量预测，盈亏平衡点对系统和主干高度敏感，且没有任何系统在成本和准确率两个维度上同时占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长对话智能体日益依赖记忆系统以避免每轮重发整个对话，然而记忆系统的服务成本有多大缺乏系统性的基准测试，导致实际部署时难以做成本评估和选型决策。

Method: 对比三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测记忆）与两种参考策略（固定尺寸滚动窗口、重发完整转录），在两种主干模型上进行最长400轮对话的测试，每个成本测量均搭配665个LoCoMo问题的回答准确率。

Result: 三项关键发现：（1）记忆系统的服务成本无法仅由对话长度和消息大小预测，成本回归模型对记忆系统的预测偏差达18-69%，成本实际由内部记忆行为驱动；（2）盈亏平衡分析显示记忆系统相比完整转录何时更便宜高度依赖系统和主干，最便宜的系统在最初几十轮即盈亏平衡，最贵的在400轮内始终不划算；（3）没有任何系统在成本和准确率两个维度同时胜出，准确率范围21-54%，且主干选择对成本的影响与记忆系统本身相当。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的服务成本具有高度系统特异的内部行为特征，无法以简单的外在场因素直接预测；其经济性与准确率之间存在权衡，主干模型的选择对成本影响重大。这一系统性的基准结果为长对话记忆系统的实际选型与部署提供了重要的量化参考。

Abstract: Long-running conversational agents increasingly rely on a memory system to avoid resending the whole conversation each turn, yet how much that costs to serve has received little systematic benchmarking. We compare three memory systems (Mem0, Hindsight, and Mastra Observational Memory) against two reference strategies -- a fixed-size rolling window and resubmitting the full transcript -- across two backbones and conversations of up to 400 turns, pairing every cost measurement with answer accuracy on 665 LoCoMo questions. First, a memory system's serving cost cannot be predicted from conversation length and message size alone: a regression that tracks the two reference strategies closely misses the memory systems by 18-69%, their cost driven instead by internal memory behavior. Second, a break-even analysis shows that whether -- and when -- a memory system becomes cheaper to serve than the full transcript is highly sensitive to the system and the backbone, from the first tens of turns for the cheapest to never within 400 turns for the most expensive. Third, no system wins on both axes: accuracy spans 21-54%, and the backbone choice drives cost as much as the memory system does.

</details>


### [40] [LazyTrain: Limited-resource Allocation toward Zero-waste Yield Optimization in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11919)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LazyTrain 是在层流式执行器之上构建的优化层，将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和通信重叠统一建模为混合整数调度问题，并结合 Hybr id 8-bit 优化器状态压缩与快速梯度裁剪，在有限硬件上实现大模型训练加速约1.24×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限硬件上训练大语言模型日益成为跨 GPU 计算、主机内存、PCIe 传输和存储带宽的调度问题。现有 offloading 系统和 MegaTrain 虽能减少 GPU 驻留并实现单 GPU 大模型训练，但固定检查点策略和启发式放置仍使通信暴露在关键路径上，缺乏全局优化的调度方案。

Method: LazyTrain 将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和 CPU-GPU-NVMe 通信重叠形式化为混合整数调度问题（MIS），在训练前求解策略并执行。同时提出 Hybrid 8-bit 算子，将 8-bit 优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪结合：状态压缩降低优化器内存占用，快速裁剪抵消 CPU 端更新开销。

Result: 在 H800 上，从 Qwen2.5-3B 到 Qwen3.6-27B，LazyTrain 相比匹配基线持续 TFLOPS 提升约1.24×；RTX 3090 上最大可行批大小在各模型规模均增加一。Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA 主实验达到 219.95 TFLOPS、1361 tokens/s、GPU 内存峰值 68.84GB，完整评估集上精确匹配准确率 95.42%。

Conclusion: 通过将训练调度提升为混合整数规划问题并配合 Hybrid 8-bit 算子，LazyTrain 有效消除了通信关键路径暴露，在有限硬件上显著提升了训练吞吐和可扩展批大小，并已在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Training large language models on limited hardware is increasingly a scheduling problem across GPU compute, host memory, PCIe transfer, and storage bandwidth. Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTrain formulates checkpoint selection, activation placement, recomputation, and CPU-GPU-NVMe communication overlap as a mixed-integer scheduling problem, then executes the solved policy during training. It further couples 8-bit optimizer states with fast gradient clipping as a single Hybrid 8-bit operator: state compression reduces optimizer-state memory, while fast clipping counteracts the additional CPU-side update overhead. Across H800 experiments from Qwen2.5-3B to Qwen3.6-27B, LazyTrain improves sustained TFLOPS over matched baselines runs by approximately 1.24$\times$; RTX 3090 experiments likewise increase the maximum feasible batch size by one at each model scale. In the primary Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA run, LazyTrain reaches 219.95 TFLOPS and 1361 tokens/s at batch size 72, peaks at 68.84\,GB of GPU memory, and obtains 95.42\% exact-match accuracy on the full evaluation split. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/LazyTrain.

</details>


### [41] [LODESTAR: Trustworthy Entropy Is Navigated, Not Merely Measured -- Reinforced Polarizer Keeps a Frozen LLM from Being Confidently Misled by the Wrong Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11922)
*Po-Jen Ko,Che-Cheng Wu,Hung-Chun Hsu,Li-Yang Chang,Chuan-Ju Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LODESTAR，一种通过强化学习训练"极化器"（短自然语言字符串）插入到冻结的响应LLM提示中，以诱导其在检索增强问答中选择正确答案的方法，在5,008个问题上取得最高F1（0.5339）和精确匹配（0.4136），并显著超越所有已发布的选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然预测分布熵（选择最低回答熵的候选答案）是检索增强问答中强大的选择规则，能显著提升F1，但最低熵规则存在系统性失败：误导性段落会使响应模型自信地给出错误答案，熵下降的地方恰恰是最不可信的信号。作者发现失败源于响应模型阅读的段落，而该上下文是可以人工干预的输入，从而提出通过文本干预来弥补熵选择规则的缺陷。

Method: LODESTAR训练一个"极化器"——一段短的自然语言字符串，插入到冻结的响应LLM的提示中而非其权重中。训练采用强化学习（RL），离线进行一次，训练标签由gold答案和两个LLM评判器离线构建，推理时不读取这些标签。极化器通过评分文本干预在第三方冻结响应模型中引发的不确定性（对比同一问题的候选答案之间的不确定性）来选择最佳回答。

Result: 在5,008个问题（五个QA基准）上，LODESTAR取得最高平均F1（0.5339，对比最低熵规则的0.5148和检索器排名第一段落的0.4769），最高精确匹配（0.4136），最高GPT-4o评判分数（0.6435）。三种子均值在70个方法×数据集F1单元中全部获胜，对每个基线保持配对显著性。增益在领域内和领域外均成立。消融实验表明极化器使响应模型减少读取误导性段落的频率（26.0%对30.3%）。

Conclusion: LODESTAR作为首个通过文本干预（而非权重修改）影响冻结响应模型不确定性来改进检索增强问答选择的方法，验证了"读取的上下文是可干预输入"这一核心假设，并通过插入极化器使响应模型更少被误导性段落带偏，从而在全评测维度上超越了所有现有选择器。

Abstract: Predictive-distribution entropy makes a strong selection rule in retrieval-augmented question answering: across five QA benchmarks, keeping the candidate answer that a frozen respondent LLM produces with the lowest answer-token entropy lifts mean answer $F_1$ from 0.4769 to 0.5148 over the retriever's top-ranked passage, with no gold answers. Yet this lowest-entropy rule, which prior entropy-based selectors adopt, fails in a specific and consequential way: a misleading passage makes the respondent confidently wrong, driving its entropy down precisely where the signal looks most trustworthy. We show that the failure comes from the passage the respondent reads -- and the context that passage is read in is an input we can intervene on. We introduce LODESTAR, to our knowledge the first method to score a text intervention by the uncertainty it induces in a third-party frozen respondent, compared across one question's candidates. LODESTAR uses reinforcement learning to train, once and offline, a polarizer -- a short fixed natural-language string inserted into the respondent's prompt and never into its weights; its training labels are built offline from gold answers and two LLM judges, and inference reads neither. Evaluating every competing selector under the same frozen respondent and the same candidate pools on 5,008 questions, LODESTAR attains the highest mean $F_1$ of any inference-ready selector (0.5148 to 0.5339), the highest exact match (0.4136), and the highest GPT-4o judge score of the frozen-respondent configurations judged (0.6435); its three-seed mean wins all 70 method-by-dataset $F_1$ cells against fourteen published configurations while remaining paired-significant against every one. The gain holds both in-domain and out-of-domain, and ablating the polarizer shows it is what makes the respondent read a misleading passage less often (26.0% against 30.3%).

</details>


### [42] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Spark-to-Paper是一个端到端研究论文生成系统，由13个可组合技能组成，部署在现有编码助手中，无需独立代理平台。它将模型判断与确定性操作分离，并将实验规划与报告分离，实现了99.5%的引用有效性、96.4%的图表可编辑性，每篇论文成本$8.1，平均耗时3.2小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将研究想法转化为完整论文不仅需要文本生成，还涉及文献检索、实验设计与执行、依据证据修订声明、生成可发表图表以及在整个长生成过程中保持一致性。现有系统难以在长研究流程中保持可靠性和证据中心性。

Method: Spark-to-Paper实现了13个可组合技能，核心设计包括：(1) 将模型判断与可执行可检验的确定性操作分离；(2) 将实验规划与报告分离，先明确所需证据再观察结果；(3) 结合确定性完整性检查和自我批判，界定并约束"自我反驳循环"失败模式；(4) 通过程序化绘图和基于代码的重建生成可编辑矢量图。

Result: 在8个受控研究主题上，引用有效性99.5%，图表可编辑性96.4%。消融实验将伪造检测率从单次草稿的14%提升至完整栈的92%，对抗性评审精度达74%。全系统每篇论文消耗11.9M tokens，成本$8.1，平均3.2小时完成。

Conclusion: 端到端研究论文生成可作为轻量级、可组合的工作流在现有编码助手中实现，同时保持实验证据对声明接受、修订或放弃的中央地位。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [43] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模语言模型在多选题中因选项顺序产生的位置偏差问题。研究发现，通过遮挡选项标签（generation-then-matching和孤立评分）两种策略均无法可靠地消除位置偏差或提升准确率；唯一能匹配基线性能的配置是向模型展示所有选项并配合LLM匹配器。但即使完全消除位置影响，也不一定能带来准确率提升，而循环排列选项反而常常能改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选题基准被广泛用于评估大语言模型，但多选题分数将知识与对选项顺序的敏感度混为一谈，使其成为不可靠的知识度量指标。作者希望测试能否通过防止模型在作答时看到选项标签来消除位置影响，从而提升模型性能，提供更可靠的知识评估手段。

Method: 作者测试了两种缓解位置偏差的策略：(1) 生成答案后匹配（generation-then-matching），即模型先不看到选项标签生成答案，再与选项匹配；(2) 孤立评分各选项（scoring options in isolation），该方法在构建上天然无位置偏差。此外，作者还进行了完整的分解分析，以区分是遮挡选项造成的瓶颈还是匹配步骤造成的瓶颈，并考察了不同配置（如全选项配合LLM匹配器、循环排列）的效果。

Result: 两种缓解策略都无法可靠提升准确率。分解分析表明瓶颈在于不向模型展示选项，而非匹配步骤。唯一能一致匹配基线的配置是向模型展示所有选项并配合LLM匹配器。完全消除位置偏差并不能可靠地带来准确率提升，而循环排列选项反而经常改善性能。此外，两阶段提示中，召回不平衡的聚合度量和对顺序敏感性的每问题直接度量都无法可靠地证明去偏效果。

Conclusion: 研究者应避免假设消除位置偏差会自动带来准确率提升。遮挡选项引入的信息瓶颈超过了去偏带来的收益；循环排列可能是比去偏更有效的实用策略。现有去偏方法缺乏可靠的验证度量，需要重新审视多选题评估框架中知识与位置敏感度的关系。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>

*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对小型语言模型（SLMs）的可信度进行了全面评估，涵盖公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理等多个维度，发现量化比剪枝更能保持可信度，且从可靠大模型压缩得到的SLMs优于从头训练的小模型，知识蒸馏可进一步提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs因其高效性在资源受限场景中备受关注，但现有研究主要聚焦于训练效率和压缩技术，对如何构建可信的SLMs这一问题缺乏系统探索。随着语言模型在实际应用中的广泛部署，可信度已成为关键问题，因此需要全面评估SLM在公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理等方面的表现。

Method: 论文采用多维度评估框架，系统比较了两种主流SLM构建路径（从头训练与压缩预训练模型），并具体考察了剪枝和量化两种压缩技术对模型可信度的影响，同时探究了知识蒸馏在提升SLM可信度方面的作用。

Result: 研究发现：（1）量化相比剪枝能更有效地保留模型可信度；（2）通过量化压缩可靠的大语言模型所得SLM在可信度和适应性上优于从头训练的小模型；（3）从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏可进一步增强SLM的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建可信小型语言模型提供了实践指导，明确指出压缩路径（尤其是量化）是比从头训练更优的选择，并为未来可信SLM的研发与部署奠定了研究基础。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [45] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A-CRC-QA，一个后验校准框架，用于不确定性感知的选择性问答，将选择条件下的错误控制重构为线性期望约束，并通过单调化经验风险校准过程实现渐近风险控制，无需额外训练，可搭配不同不确定性估计器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能生成流畅但不正确的答案，因此不确定性量化对可靠问答至关重要。然而，启发式不确定性分数无法完美区分正确与错误预测，且直接应用固定不确定性阈值无法对已接受答案的错误率提供统计控制。

Method: 将选择条件下的错误控制重构为线性期望约束，应用基于共形风险控制的单调化经验风险校准程序。由于实例级损失关于接受阈值通常非单调，该方法针对渐近而非有限样本风险控制。框架是模型无关的，无需额外训练，可与不同不确定性估计器结合。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA上的实验表明，该方法适用于开放式和封闭式问答，相比未校准和基于置信界的方法，在已接受答案的可信度和答案保留率之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA为LLM选择性问答提供了一种统计上有保证的后验校准方案，在保证接受答案可靠性的同时保持较高的答案保留率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>

*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的孟加拉语多方言神经机器翻译系统，支持12种区域方言之间的多向翻译，绕过了标准语中转。系统构建了迄今最大的孟加拉语多方言平行语料库（51,531句对，含2,500句专家验证的新增双向句对），使用DoRA微调的BanglaT5模型取得了SOTA效果（29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++），并部署为INT8量化的开源Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语拥有超过2.4亿使用者，存在显著的区域方言差异，在音系、形态和词汇上均与标准口语孟加拉语（SCB）差异明显。现有的NMT架构和LLM主要假设语言同质分布，导致低资源区域方言的翻译性能严重退化。因此需要构建一个能够直接进行多方言多向翻译而无需中间标准语中转的系统。

Method: 1) 构建迄今最大的孟加拉语多方言平行语料库，涵盖12种方言共51,531个非空平行句对，其中包含2,500个由专家验证、针对五种此前未被研究方言的双向平行句对；2) 在权重分解低秩适配（DoRA）下微调序列到序列架构模型（BanglaT5）；3) 进行系统的跨方言迁移分析与数据集扩展研究，建立低资源方言适应的经验阈值；4) 将INT8量化后的模型部署为开源Web应用。

Result: 微调后的BanglaT5模型在翻译性能上达到SOTA水平（29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++），优于NLLB-200（615M）和mBART-50（611M），同时保持了形态连贯性。系统支持12种方言的多向翻译，无需通过标准语中转。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了DoRA微调下的BanglaT5在孟加拉语多方言翻译上的有效性，同时通过与模型规模相当的对比模型的比较，确立了其性能优势。开放的数据集和量化部署方案有助于促进边缘化方言社区的数字包容性。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [47] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好树优化（PTO）框架，通过带前瞻的偏好树（Preference Tree with Look-Ahead）生成偏好数据，结合直接偏好优化（DPO）迭代提升目标导向对话系统中的智能体模型，并在动机访谈（MI）领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发支持多轮、目标导向对话的对话系统仍面临重大挑战，尤其是在数据稀缺的专业领域。现有方法缺乏有效的迭代改进机制，难以在有限数据条件下提升智能体的决策能力。

Method: 提出PTO框架：1) 利用虚拟患者和oracle评估器模拟对话，通过Preference Tree with Look-Ahead方法生成丰富的偏好数据集；2) 将该方法与直接偏好优化（DPO）结合，在迭代训练循环中增强智能体的决策能力；3) 引入前瞻模拟（look-ahead simulations）以改善长期规划。

Result: 实验表明：PTO框架显著提升了对话智能体在动机访谈领域目标导向对话中的表现；经PTO训练的模型在会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标上持续优于基线；加入前瞻模拟改善了长期规划和对话策略有效性，更深的前瞻配置取得了最稳定且得分最高的结果。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了目标导向对话系统中的数据稀缺问题，推动了对话系统在专业目标导向领域的发展，为构建更细致和有效的对话系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [48] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了NLP中与"视角"（perspective）相关的各类概念（如立场、情感、框架、论点等），提出一组属性来区分这些概念，并构建了一个线性层次结构以帮助研究者选择与其研究目标相匹配的概念操作化方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一事件可能因作者的经验、背景和信念不同而以不同视角被报道。NLP多个领域都涉及视角分析，但现有研究使用了大量操作概念（立场、情感、框架、论点等），这些概念之间的精确关系尚不明确，缺乏深层次的理论理解会阻碍视角相关研究的开展。

Method: 作者系统回顾了NLP中"视角"的研究空间，定义了一组用于区分视角相关概念的属性，并基于这些属性提出一个线性层次结构，将这些概念沿单一轴组织排列，以阐明它们之间的关系。

Result: 提出一个有原则的概念层次结构，将视角相关的各概念沿单一维度线性排列，并展示了该层次结构如何帮助研究者导航该领域，选择与其具体研究目标一致的概念操作化方案。

Conclusion: 该理论框架填补了视角相关概念之间关系不清的研究空白，为NLP研究者提供了一个系统的概念分类和选择指南，有望促进视角分析研究的理论深化和实践规范化。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [49] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QV-PIC框架，通过查询感知的双分辨率缓存重用机制，解决渲染图像PIC（Position-Independent Caching）在RAG中质量退化的问题，在不牺牲效率的前提下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在跨查询时重复预填充相同文本块，造成冗余计算。PIC通过重用KV缓存缓解了这一问题，但纯文本PIC受限于大量文本token的效率瓶颈。将文本渲染为图像可压缩token数量，但渲染图像PIC相比文本PIC存在更严重的质量退化，这源于独立编译缓存间的上下文不匹配以及视觉压缩过程中细粒度文本证据的丢失。现有方法只能通过在线重计算解决前者，无法恢复丢失的文本细节。

Method: QV-PIC是一个查询感知的双分辨率PIC重用框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 离线阶段，使用模型原生聊天模板前缀编译视觉缓存，在不引入在线重计算的情况下提升PIC质量；(2) 在线阶段，用低分辨率保留全局上下文，通过累积查询相关性分数在有限的高分辨率预算内恢复细粒度文本证据，保持视觉压缩的效率优势。

Result: 在六个任务上，QV-PIC相比基础渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6点，接近基础文本PIC的性能，并超过优化后的文本PIC 2.58 F1，同时将TTFT降低17.2%。相对于完整预填充，TTFT降低83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC通过查询感知的双分辨率策略和模型原生模板引导，有效弥合了渲染图像PIC与文本PIC之间的质量差距，同时保留了视觉压缩带来的效率优势，证明了结合图像压缩与智能缓存重用在RAG中的可行性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [50] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAG（SQL-检索增强生成）架构，通过将文档组织为事件-实体索引（而非构建全局知识图谱），将每个文本块表示为语义完整的事件与其实体的配对，形成保持n元关系的潜超边。查询时以共享实体为连接键动态连接相关块，形成查询范围内的邻域，且所有证据始终保留原始块。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上均取得最优检索和端到端QA性能，随推理链复杂度增加优势扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流稠密检索方法在处理结构化约束和多跳推理方面存在固有局限；而基于图的检索方法虽能解决这些问题，但存在语义碎片化、维护成本高、增量更新复杂等缺陷。作者旨在设计一种无需构建全局知识图谱的结构化检索架构。

Method: SAG将每个文本块表示为事件（event）+实体（entity）的配对，形成潜超边（latent hyperedge），保持n元关系而不分解为三元组；查询时将共享实体作为连接键动态关联相关块，生成查询范围邻域，全程使用原始块作为证据。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个基准上均获得最佳检索和端到端QA性能，且增益随推理链复杂度增加而扩大。在MuSiQue上达到80.36% Recall@5，比最强基线高出11.52个百分点。

Conclusion: SAG为LLM智能体在持续增长的组织知识上进行检索和推理开辟了新路径，证明了无需全局知识图谱即可实现结构化多跳检索的可行性。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [51] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了VITA——一个专为印度等中低收入国家（LMIC）情境构建的检索增强生成（RAG）临床系统。在4,023道HealthBench问题上，VITA以51.9%的评分位居第一，领先于GPT-5.4等前沿大语言模型；在500题子集上使用更新的模型和独立裁判复测后，VITA与GPT-5.5达到统计上不可区分的水平。结果表明，领域专用RAG系统在开放式基准上仍可与前沿LLM竞争，语料特异性提升了grounding但牺牲了部分沟通流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对通用LLM与专用临床AI系统的对比研究基于高收入国家的基准和有限系统集，缺乏对中低收入国家（LMIC）情境下临床AI能力的实证评估。本文旨在检验一个专为印度等资源受限环境构建的RAG系统在面对前沿通用LLM时的竞争力。

Method: VITA是一个检索增强生成系统，从精选语料库中检索信息，包括疾病专用指南、印度特异性抗菌药物耐药数据、国家处方集约束和资源受限护理方案。作者使用GPT-4.1作为裁判对4,023道（占80.5%）英文HealthBench问题进行评分；随后选取500题子集，使用当前世代模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.5 Pro、Grok 4.3）和与所有测试系统无谱系关联的开放权重裁判DeepSeek-V4-Pro进行复测。基准、医生撰写的评分量规及完整输出均公开供独立验证。

Result: 在4,023题上VITA以51.9%评分排名第一，超越GPT-5.4（46.1%）、o4-mini（44.3%）、Gemini 3.1 Pro（42.6%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（37.3%），并在45.4%的题目上得分最高。在500题新模型复测中，差距收窄至持平：VITA与GPT-5.5在平均每题得分上统计不可区分，但VITA在加权总分和获胜题数上领先。在中立裁判下，VITA的准确性和完整性优势保持，但沟通类评分较低。

Conclusion: 专为特定临床情境构建的RAG系统在开放式医学基准上仍可与前沿通用LLM保持竞争力。语料特异性作为设计变量提升了回答的grounding（准确性、完整性），但以降低沟通流畅度为代价。该工作强调在中低收入国家情境下评估临床AI的重要性，并通过公开基准和评分输出保证结果的可独立验证性。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [52] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了层交错混合线性注意力（HLA）大语言模型中的"大规模激活"（MAs），发现两种与架构对齐的形态：全注意力层前的预注意力尖峰（PAS）和可穿过线性注意力层的尖峰间平台（ISP）。该组织在五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域以及1.2B到397B参数的模型中普遍存在，并通过受控预训练和系统异常值分析揭示了其共享的生命周期机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活是LLM中的重要现象，但此前研究主要聚焦于纯全注意力LLM，对于层交错混合线性注意力（HLA）架构中的MA形态及其产生机制缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 对五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域及1.2B到397B参数的开放混合模型进行系统分析；在高达1.3B参数的GDN混合架构上做受控预训练，考察输出门控对MA形态的影响；通过系统性异常值分析来揭示MA的共享生命周期机制。

Result: 发现MAs在全注意力层之前规律性产生预注意力尖峰（PAS），并能穿过线性注意力层形成尖峰间平台（ISP）；全注意力层越密集，PAS与ISP连接越紧密，最终恢复纯全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态。两种形态在预训练早期即出现，对输出门控呈现不对称响应：全注意力输出门控显著衰减MA绝对幅度但不消除层间组织，移除GDN门控仅产生适度放大。

Conclusion: MA的形态由MA取消时机决定：PAS遵循局部"写入-汇入-取消"过程，ISP的持久性与延迟取消一致；在全注意力极限下该机制恢复纯全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态，为理解HLA架构中MA的涌现与演化提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [53] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息充裕悖论"（Information Abundance Paradox），指出训练上下文中丰富的相关信息反而会降低模型将信息参数化内化的动机，增加对上下文的依赖。预训练中增大上下文窗口仅在中间最优值处提升性能，之后持续下降；监督微调中更多的训练时上下文提升有支持条件下的表现，但降低上下文缺失或误导时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越倾向于使用长上下文进行训练和部署，其隐含假设是更长的上下文暴露更多丰富证据只会对模型有益。作者挑战这一观点，试图研究上下文窗口如何塑造模型的学习模式，即在参数化内化与上下文化之间如何权衡，从而揭示长上下文训练可能带来的弊端。

Method: 作者通过以下方式验证假设：1）在预训练阶段，使用长文档系统地改变上下文窗口，评估语言建模、自然语言理解和闭卷多选问答的性能；2）在监督微调阶段，操纵训练时上下文的丰富程度，测试有支持上下文、无支持和误导上下文情况下的表现；3）通过机制分析，追踪训练信号在注意力模块与前馈网络（与参数化知识相关）之间的梯度分配；4）采用因果干预实验验证模型对上下文依赖程度的转变。

Result: 预训练中上下文窗口增大时，性能在中间某个最优值处达到峰值后持续下降；监督微调中更多任务相关的训练时上下文改善有支持上下文下的性能，但降低测试时无上下文或误导上下文时的鲁棒性。机制上，信息丰富的上下文训练将梯度压力从前馈网络转移到注意力模块，因果干预证实这增加了推理时的上下文依赖。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持信息充裕悖论，表明追求近无限上下文并非简单地提供更多数据即可，即使高质量长上下文数据充足，长上下文训练也会从根本上改变模型的知识存储和利用方式，可能导致参数化知识的不足和上下文依赖的过度。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [54] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于单一大语言模型模拟用户行为的多智能体强化学习方法存在系统性的泛化失败问题，根源在于模拟器坍缩（simulator collapse）。作者从理论上形式化该问题，并提出两种互补解法：推理期的Verbalized Sampling和训练期的Co-Training，在三个多轮对话基准及真人实验中验证了有效性，同时开源了SCOPE框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互领域的多智能体强化学习通常仅依赖单一LLM模拟用户行为，但这种方式在泛化到未见模拟器和真实用户时表现不佳。作者追溯其原因在于模拟器LLM的模式坍缩导致策略过拟合到单一主导模式，从而无法迁移泛化。这一系统性缺陷此前未被理论化研究和系统性解决。

Method: 作者首先从理论上形式化模拟器坍缩问题；随后提出两种互补解法：(1) 推理期方案Verbalized Sampling——从口头化的响应分布中采样以拓宽模拟器的行为空间，缓解模式坍缩；(2) 训练期方案Co-Training——联合优化策略对抗一组可训练的模拟器群体，防止策略过度拟合任何单一模拟器的模式。实验在Persuasion for Good、τ²-bench和CooperBench三个多轮基准上进行验证。

Result: Verbalized Sampling相比单一模拟器RL在留出集成功率上提升最高9%，Co-Training进一步提升至14%；真人研究显示在真实用户上也取得了类似的增益。两种方法均保留了单一模拟器RL下会坍缩的策略多样性。

Conclusion: 训练环境的多样性（而不仅是策略本身）对多轮RL向真实世界部署的泛化至关重要。该工作为人口级协同训练多智能体RL提供了理论基础、实践方案和开源框架SCOPE。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [55] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合建模框架，结合无监督聚类（GMM）与有监督分类（Random Forest），利用自然语言处理技术分析厄瓜多尔公共采购系统（SOCE）中承包商在合同前阶段生成的评论文本，以检测潜在的不合规或举报式评论。实验表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入配合GMM聚类与Random Forest分类器在严重类别不平衡条件下仍能取得高精度和高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及大量财政资源，需要持续审计与监督，但利益相关者评论和公开政府数据往往未被充分利用。现有方法未能有效挖掘这些文本数据中蕴含的程序性违规线索，因此需要一种轻量级、可落地的NLP框架来识别潜在的不合规采购流程。

Method: 提出一个混合建模框架：1）使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入；2）采用高斯混合模型（GMM）进行无监督聚类以发现潜在模式；3）应用有监督分类阶段识别指控式或举报式评论。整个流程集成在一个NLP流水线中。

Result: 领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入与GMM聚类及Random Forest分类器组合，在严重类别不平衡条件下实现了高精度和高召回率，验证了轻量级领域自适应NLP架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 轻量级、领域自适应的NLP架构能够有效支持公共采购系统中的风险识别与透明度提升，无需大规模计算基础设施即可在实际场景中部署应用。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [56] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过孟加拉语案例，揭示了AI教育工具在欠发达地区部署时面临的四重结构性障碍——网络内容缺口、训练数据不足、分词惩罚和连接性排斥，主张将数据稀缺视为结构性壁垒而非孤立的技术限制，并提出离线优先设计作为面向公平的基础设施策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具被广泛视为解决欠发达地区教育资源匮乏的可扩展方案，但底层基础设施（训练语料、分词方案、评估基准和部署架构）在模型训练前就已系统性地边缘化使用不足语言的群体。作者希望揭示这一被忽视的结构性问题。

Method: 以孟加拉语为案例，量化分析四类结构性障碍：1) 网络内容占比（<0.5% vs 近4%的人口占比）；2) 主流多语言语料库中英语-孟加拉语的训练token缺口（67:1）；3) 孟加拉语字母音节文字（alphasyllabary）分词机制导致的高token生成率惩罚；4) 城乡互联网渗透率差异（农村36.5% vs 城市71.4%）。

Result: 识别出四个相互关联的结构性失败：网络内容代表性严重不足、67:1的训练token赤字、分词效率惩罚加剧数据缺口、以及低连接环境中的连接性排斥。这些问题的根源在于长期的资源分配决策、制度优先事项和未将不足语言置于核心的设计默认值。

Conclusion: 数据集稀缺应被理解为结构性壁垒而非孤立的技术限制；离线优先设计应被视为面向公平的基础设施策略。论文最后提出语言学和AI研究的未来方向，以减轻这些结构性不平等。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>

### [57] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Experience Orchestrator (EO)——一个基于控制理论的治理层，用于协调目标对立的两方LLM智能体之间的多轮交互。在6万次金融客服模拟中，EO将高意图顾问联系率提升了32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），其中Contextual Bandit策略选择解释了97%的指标间方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个目标结构对立的LLM智能体进行多轮交互时，缺乏共享目标函数会导致对话崩溃而非竞争：访客屈服、站点智能体停止策略变化、对话在没有达成任何一方目标的情况下终止。作者旨在探究控制论治理层能否替代缺失的目标函数。

Method: EO通过三个机制治理联合轨迹：1）Contextual Bandit (CB) 基于真实Web分析数据选择内容臂；2）PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制执行行为一致性；3）POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。实验在模拟金融服务环境中进行，共6万次模拟。

Result: EO将高意图顾问联系率从46.1%提升至78.1%（+32个百分点）。CB变体选择解释了因子间结果方差的97%，证实治理策略而非环境初始条件决定了轨迹终点。个体层面分析揭示了两个不同机制：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是系统功能与否的分界线；对已接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的移情默认行为已基本足够。

Conclusion: 控制论治理层可以替代缺失的共享目标函数，在LLM对LLM模拟条件下显著改善目标对立智能体的多轮交互结果。但PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [58] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Distribird，一个智能体web应用，通过多智能体pipeline自动从科学文献中提取参数值并构建贝叶斯先验分布，在无文献可用时回退到无信息先验，并提供证据回溯、置信度报告和本地推理保障。评估显示其在24个参数、10个科学领域上与单提示LLM基线相当，但在可追溯性、有效性验证和隐私保护上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于过程的贝叶斯模型校准需要每个参数的先验分布，但研究人员几乎总是退回到均匀先验，因为从科学文献中构建有信息先验既慢又需要领域和统计双重专业知识。这一痛点促使作者开发自动化工具来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体pipeline：搜索文献、提取报告值并按领域相关性加权、通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布；无文献时回退到合理的无信息先验；每个先验都追溯到具体论文和数值；内置有效性层拒绝超出范围请求；所有语言模型调用均在本地运行，仅搜索词发送至公共文献数据库。

Result: 在24个参数、10个科学领域中，使用三个开源模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B）与单提示LLM基线对比，完整pipeline在先验质量上与基线持平，但单提示基线在30个模型-参数组合中的11个对超出范围请求给出了自信但无根据的先验，而Distribird的有效性层正确拒绝；且所有模型调用均在本地执行。

Conclusion: 对于科学用途，先验的可追溯性、有效性验证和隐私保护等比点估计准确率的边际提升更重要。Distribird在这些关键属性上优于单提示基线，为其在实际科学工作流中的部署提供了有力论据。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [59] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了由自主AI研究代理对Conway 99图问题（srg(99,14,1,2)是否存在）的系统性、可复现的攻击。主要贡献包括：证明循环图最多满足68.0%约束；通过强制结构约化将存在性归结为84个顶点上的12-正则图；建立验证过的预置自同构轨道存在性框架；最终最佳验证工件达69.43%，并有多重证据表明这是稳健的前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway 99图问题是组合数学中著名的开放问题，即判断参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在。该问题长期以来悬而未决，作者利用自主AI研究代理进行系统性攻击，并遵循该赛道部分信用评分指标，旨在提供可验证的部分进展和新的结构化约化，即使不能完全解决该问题，也能提供有价值的数学证据和工具。

Method: 方法包括四部分：(1) 对Z/99上的循环图枚举所有49个差分类，穷举证明最多33个差分类可满足（68.0%），同一上界对另一个99阶交换群也成立；(2) 强制结构约化：由λ=1导出每个邻域为完美匹配，μ=2将外部顶点与非匹配邻居对建立双射，将存在性问题归结为84个顶点上的12-正则图，编码到CP-SAT求解器，并通过恢复唯一的srg(9,4,1,2)验证该编码；(3) 建立预置自同构轨道存在性框架，涵盖无不动点和单不动点作用，在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上验证；(4) 最佳验证工件达到69.43%，通过十四种不同方法交叉验证其稳健性。

Result: 核心结果包括：循环图约束满足上限68.0%（33/49个差分类）；将原问题约化为12-正则84顶点图的存在性问题，并通过已知的srg(9,4,1,2)验证了编码正确性；建立了可验证的预置自同构框架；最佳工件69.43%超过循环图上限但低于满分，且十四种独立方法均未超过该值，表明这是一个稳健的算法前沿。

Conclusion: 作者指出，任何可证明的低于4950的约束上界本身就是非存在性证明，因此69.43%的最佳工件与开放问题紧密关联。虽然未解决Conway 99图问题，但本文提供了可复现的穷举结果、新颖的结构约化（将问题规模从99顶点降至84个12-正则图的12-正则图）以及验证过的自同构框架，这些构成了对该问题有意义的实质性进展和部分信用。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [60] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k混合专家（MoE）路由的不连续性如何放大KV缓存量化损伤。通过四轮系统性装置（路由介导分数RMF、token级归因、预注册探测），在三个架构上发现约三分之一的4-bit KV量化损伤是由路由翻转介导的。可部署的路由器边际能检测到翻转发生（AUC 0.772），但无法区分有害翻转与有益翻转——在可观测的路由器统计特征族内，没有任何预测器能超越随机水平判断翻转的损失符号，构成经验性的效益检测屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由是离散不连续的，因此部署中数值扰动（如KV缓存量化）可能将token推过决策边界，改变被激活的专家集合。这种路由介导的损伤与直接数值精度损失造成的损伤在机制上不同，但此前缺乏系统的因果归因装置来区分这两种损伤路径，也缺少对"路由翻转"的检测与修复可行性的实证评估。

Method: 提出四轮运行装置：(1) 利用受保护的BF16门控模拟4-bit KV量化读取，通过路由介导分数（RMF）度量量化损伤中经路由翻转介导的比例；(2) token级归因将损伤按机制分解；(3) 预注册探针在OLMoE-1B-7B、另外两个模型架构上验证结论；(4) 引入可部署的路由器边际统计量检测翻转并评估其预测翻转损失符号的能力。辅以受控同检点标志交换实验重界定门控归一化约定的作用，以及真实int4 KV内核验证。

Result: OLMoE-1B-7B在4-bit KV下RMF约0.31（发现集0.31 [0.20, 0.41]；过程复制均值0.313±0.020；预注册重执行0.231）。路由器边际可检测翻转（AUC 0.772）但无法判别翻转的有害性（接近随机）。有符号翻转税与符号不可分离性跨模型成立；干净参考补救的收益因架构而异；门归一化约定被重界定为损伤幅度调节因子而非路由可恢复性机制。真实int4内核的分数与假量化剂量曲线兼容但统计功效不足（95% CI [-0.111, 0.394]含零）。

Conclusion: MoE路由翻转构成KV量化损伤中不可忽视的组成部分（约1/3），但当前可观测的路由器统计量在有益/有害翻转判别上存在经验性检测屏障，限制了仅针对此特征族的选择性修复方案。本文不提出新缓解措施，而是为路由介导损伤提供因果分析框架、实证基准和检测极限边界，为后续工作设定严谨的预注册方法论先例。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [61] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用低参数代理模型替代大规模LLM智能体社会模拟的方法，通过统计物理观察，用几百到几千次廉价查询拟合每个智能体，从而能在笔记本上运行任意N规模的社会模拟，并通过交互序×记忆的分类法预测代理误差的N趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM智能体社会模拟计算成本高昂，但实际关心的问题通常是宏观层面的（相变行为、典型事实、随N的标度关系），而非单个智能体的认知细节。因此需要一种低成本但能保持宏观统计性质的方法。

Method: （1）将每个LLM智能体替换为从数百到数千次廉价查询中拟合的低参数模型；（2）引入[交互序×记忆]分类法，将感知和记忆映射到有效理论，并预测代理误差随N的变化趋势；（3）在忠实复现的EconAgent宏观经济模拟及其他七个命名LLM模拟上进行验证，使用真实LLM（主要为DeepSeek）引发的智能体决策克隆，成本仅数美元。

Result: 预测的误差趋势在每个分类单元中都成立；两个被反驳的预测（均出现在强饱和响应且源于其曲率的情况）也被无自由参数的理论定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法可行且可预测：是否有效在模拟运行前即可判定，主要取决于各智能体的感知属性；代理方法的有效性可通过理论框架在运行前预判。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [62] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试，用于评估前沿AI编码代理在开放研究任务（而非工程规范任务）中自主改进世界模型的能力。基准涵盖8个游戏环境，采用统一的张量状态表示，在64次会话中代理在63次中取得改进，其中91%的改进是非平凡的研究型修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模领域缺乏统一定式，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间存在复杂交互，理想的改进方向不预先明确，这与当前代理基准测试中主导的"按规范工程"任务不同。作者希望提供一种测试平台，使前沿编码代理能在开放研究而非工程规范问题上被评估。

Method: 构建AutoWorldModel-Bench闭环基准：跨越8个游戏环境，统一使用从每个游戏中提取的真实实体状态作为结构化状态表示，并通过共享张量格式传递给模型，从而将动力学建模与感知解耦，实现快速迭代（分钟级每轮）。代理在固定计算预算下自主改进提供的世界模型初始版本。

Result: 在64次会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次会话中改进了初始模型；其中91%的会话中获胜修改是非平凡的研究风格改动——新目标函数、新表示、新滚动过程或架构变更——而非超参数调整。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了一个评估前沿编码代理在开放研究型任务中表现的测试平台，区别于传统的engineering-to-spec问题，为AI研究者自主开展科学研究的能力评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [63] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead是一种在潜空间中实现复制型潜在存储选择性可读的方法：将共享的计算状态以键值缓存片段形式存储，通过无冲突复制数据类型合并，利用基于词汇的键标签集路由和硬注意力掩蔽解码，使后续查询能够可靠地读取已访问的片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立智能体在潜空间中推理时可以共享计算状态（以键值缓存片段形式而非文本），并通过无冲突复制数据类型(CRDT)合并为收敛的存储。但核心问题是：后来的查询在编码时未知，无法可靠读取合并后的缓存——共置片段会相互干扰，共置不等于可寻址性。

Method: MaSRead采用内容寻址方案：(1) 通过从片段词汇派生的不透明键控标签集合进行路由；(2) 对每个选中片段在硬注意力掩码下解码，隐藏其余片段；(3) 利用词汇连通性，通过图遍历到达多跳查询所需的片段。解码时仅依赖片段长度而非存储总规模，端到端工作量包括存储相关的路由和每个访问片段的一次读取。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、枢纽和自然语言存储等多类场景中，MaSRead能够在隔离状态下恢复已访问片段，在无关片段累积时仍保持有效性，并能迁移到另一模型家族。物化解码依赖片段长度而非总存储大小。

Conclusion: MaSRead证明复制型潜在存储可以通过内容连接实现选择性可读。局限性明确：词汇路由可能错过无连接证据，答案组合受限于冻结的读取器。因此当所需片段通过内容与查询连接时，后续查询即可对复制潜在存储进行选择性读取。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [64] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAPO，一种段级自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式等段，基于top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性改进，在多个基准数据集上超越现有APO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化（APO）方法通常以整体方式重写提示，可能导致改进一个行为的同时损害其他行为，缺乏细粒度的优化粒度。

Method: 将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个段；优化循环使用单个LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出，依次完成分段、弱点分析和候选生成；采用两阶段生成流程：(1)段级诊断与推荐提取，(2)受弱/强段信号约束的候选合成，并配合训练/验证协议。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K上使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini进行评估，SAPO在平均分数上取得最佳表现，超越Zero-shot和APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA、StraGO等强APO基线。

Conclusion: 段级提示优化相比整体优化能更精准地定位问题段并进行针对性改进，在多个任务上获得更好的平均性能。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [65] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P&ID Pilot，一个端到端AI流水线，结合遗传算法（GA）和大型语言模型（LLM）实现从工艺流程框图（PFD）到管道仪表图（P&ID）的自动化生成，在两个阶段均取得了显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程框图（PFD）及其向管道仪表图（P&ID）的转换目前主要依赖人工完成，耗时且成本高。引入人工智能有望实现流程自动化、节省时间，并通过探索多种拓扑方案减少人工劳动来获得经济效益。

Method: 提出两阶段端到端AI流水线：第一阶段进行PFD综合，对比四种方法后发现GA与LLM的混合方法能生成最优且有效的PFD拓扑；第二阶段利用基于LLM的智能体，通过受限的工程软件开发工具包（SDK）对生成的PFD进行修改，将其转换为有源可溯的P&ID。

Result: 混合方法（GA+LLM）在所有方法中实现了最低的损失值，满足所需的出口流参数且无工程规则违规；LLM智能体在第二阶段实现了100%的执行成功率，同时保持与领域特定规则和参考图结构的一致性。

Conclusion: 该统一流水线将GA/LLM驱动的综合与基于LLM的转换智能体相结合，为端到端流程设计自动化提供了可行路径，能生成经过验证的可部署输出，并大幅减少人工工程工作量。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [66] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于"Influences"概念的概念框架，用于支持CPS仿真活动的迭代增量式精化，帮助开发者更好地理解系统中未建模的环境中介交互及其对系统行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS由多利益相关方开发，其行为由各领域工件与环境交互涌现。仿真和联合仿真虽已成为分析CPS行为的关键手段，但许多环境中介交互（超出直接传感和执行的交互）因复杂性、时间或领域经验不足而未被建模，导致仿真结果难以被正确理解和利用。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，引入"Influences"这一新概念，用于支持仿真活动的迭代和增量式精化，以加深对系统行为的理解。通过移动机器人案例（使用Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真）验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 通过移动机器人Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真案例研究，展示了所提出框架能够支持仿真活动的迭代精化，帮助识别和理解未建模的环境中介交互对系统行为的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Influences的概念框架为CPS仿真中的环境交互理解提供了系统化支持，能够促进开发者对系统行为的深入理解，提升仿真结果的可利用性。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [67] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以持久记忆为核心的终身AI伙伴框架，用于材料科学研究。该框架将科学经验存储为可检查的事实和可执行的技能（self-evolving memory），支持跨模型迁移、检索和修订。在49个真实材料工具使用问题、元素固体状态方程计算和13个材料仿真工作流中验证，记忆框架显著提升了任务成功率、减少错误和降低计算负担，表明agent记忆可作为超越单一模型的可持久科学资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究的进展依赖于累积的经验——可用的脚本、可信的协议、失败计算/实验的警告以及将新问题与旧结果关联的判断力。这些经验对可复现性和知识转移至关重要，但通常分散在笔记本、仓库、作业日志和个人记忆中，且很少能跨AI智能体移植。现有agent体系围绕特定模型实现设计，缺乏持久、可迁移的科学经验存储机制。

Method: 提出一个自演进记忆框架，将科学经验组织为两类组件：（1）可检查的事实（inspectable facts），如观察结果和失败边界；（2）可执行的技能（executable skills），如协议和验证检查。这些记忆可以被检索、修订并在不同模型间迁移，而不需要更新模型参数。框架在三个计算场景中评估：真实材料工具使用问题、元素固体状态方程计算和实际材料仿真工作流。

Result: （1）在49个真实材料工具使用问题（含138个可执行子任务）中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率几乎翻倍，且无需模型参数更新；（2）在元素固体状态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前护栏，结果从22/1/4提升到25/2/0（Correct/Partial/Error），避免了92%的重复错误；（3）在13个实际材料仿真工作流中，记忆技能和失败事实使聚合trace负担（token）减半，第三轮工具调用减少超过两倍，同时保持带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析的物理意义。

Conclusion: Agent记忆可作为一种持久科学资产，是可移植、自改进的材料研究经验记录，其生命周期超越任何单一模型或agent技术栈。记忆框架为AI材料科学伙伴提供了以持久记忆而非特定agent实现为核心的设计思路。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


### [68] [Identity from the Outside: A Conceptual Framework and Research Program for AI Personality Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11225)
*Luc E. Brunet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从操作层面重新定义AI"人格克隆"中的个人身份问题，将身份解构为三个被混淆的标准（目标人物保真度、类人一般性、个体性），并提出六因子分解（基质、倾向、记忆、更新动力学、情境、外生偶然性）及状态空间形式化。核心主张是一个条件性猜想：给定智能体对自身持续性的信息和自身利益后果的假设，可版本化性倾向于降低长时程不可区分性。论文在产品克隆与个体之间识别出第三种对象"代表（delegate）"，并以"气候保真度"替代轨迹保真度作为正确的长时程标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI人格克隆技术的出现使得"个人身份"这一哲学概念需要在操作层面被重新审视。作者试图绕开"意识难题"，转而通过观察者在时间跨度内对显现（manifestations）的不可区分性来定义身份，从而为评估克隆体的质量提供可操作、可实验的框架。

Method: 提出三准则区分（保真度、类人性、个体性）与六因子身份分解模型（substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies），以状态空间形式化；将不可区分性定义为1减去评判者的区分优势，并通过随机消融估计各因子的局部灵敏度。同时借用lambda演算、线性类型与双模拟（bisimulation）作类比澄清线性性的界限；引入"代表（delegate）"作为第三类对象，提出可实验研究纲领。

Result: 论文定位为概念性/纲领性工作：将现有经验文献映射到三个准则，给出六因子分解与可消融估计的实验程序；核心结果是提出"气候保真度"取代轨迹保真度作为长时程正确准则，即匹配一个人可能响应的条件分布，而非逐条模仿其历史轨迹。

Conclusion: 最佳克隆并非在所有时刻都与原主不可区分，而是在可能响应的分布意义上与原主"等同"，即克隆体应从原主的自我分歧方式中分歧。身份在操作层面不是轨迹的复现，而是条件分布的气候式匹配。

Abstract: AI "personality clones" force a re-examination of personal identity in operational terms. Setting aside the hard problem of consciousness, we approach identity through the indiscernibility of manifestations, as assessed by an observer over a duration. We distinguish three criteria that "identity" conflates: fidelity to a target person, generic human-likeness, and individuality. We propose a six-term factorization of observed identity (substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies), with a state-space formulation. Indiscernibility is defined as one minus a judge's distinguishing advantage, and the factorization's coefficients become local sensitivities estimable by randomized ablation. The central claim is a conditional conjecture: given hypotheses about the agent's information on its own persistence and about consequences bearing on its own stakes, versionability tends to degrade long-horizon indiscernibility. An analogy with lambda-calculus, linear typing, and bisimulation clarifies what linearity does and does not establish. Between product-clone and individual we identify a third object, the delegate: a task-limited, bounded-lifespan partial clone ending in a bandwidth-limited testament. We map the empirical literature onto the three criteria, propose an experimental program, and argue that the correct long-horizon criterion is not trajectory fidelity but climate fidelity: matching the conditional distribution of a person's possible responses. The best clone is the one that diverges from the original as the original would have diverged from itself.

</details>


### [69] [Cutting AI Datacenter Energy with Reinforcement Learning: Measured Power Control of LLM Training from One GPU to the Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11226)
*Eliseo Curcio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对强化学习后训练（GRPO）在GPU硬件上的功耗行为进行系统性刻画，并提出一种PPO元控制器，通过自适应调整工作负载的生成参数来管理GPU功耗。在7B规模下，该方法将功率限制违规减少89.8%，同时提升18.1%的token输出和26.2%的能效；在72B规模下发现群组大小执行器（group-size actuator）因模型分片失去权威，随后基于生成并发（generation concurrency）重建控制器，在实况72B推理负载上实现35.7%产出提升和87.2%更少的违规。文章还揭示了功耗瞬态的时间尺度效应、16-GPU集群的过订阅可行性及经济/碳影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型的后训练（RL post-training）在GPU上的功耗行为尚未被刻画，而数据中心当前采用静态上限和被动节流的盲式功耗管理手段，会无差别地拖慢硬件。需要一个能够理解并利用工作负载本身语义的功耗管理方案。

Method: 在7B、14B、72B规模、1-4块A100上以半秒级功率遥测采集GRPO训练数据（38万+样本），训练一个PPO元控制器来根据实测功率自适应调整工作负载的生成参数；通过执行器权威扫描（actuator-authority sweep）诊断模型分片导致的控制失效，并改用生成并发作为执行器重建控制器。

Result: 7B规模下：功耗上限违规减少89.8%，token输出提升18.1%，能效提升26.2%。72B初始部署为复制性无效结果，诊断后重建控制器在实况72B推理负载的三次复现中：产出比静态安全基线高35.7%，违规率为2.27±1.08%，比无控操作少87.2%违规，吞吐和能量/token均最优。时间尺度效应：23.6%的瞬态违规在半秒分辨率下，在30秒窗口降至1.6%，5分钟降为0；16-GPU组合集群在30秒+窗口零违规，峰值需求仅为额定功率的50-56%，表明约两倍名板功率过订阅可行。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知的功耗控制显著优于静态和反应式方案，但执行器的选择至关重要——控制权威取决于执行器是否在硬件分片下保有可操作空间。在合适的执行器下，GPU集群可实现显著的名板功率过订阅，兼顾吞吐、合规和能效，并带来可量化的经济和碳减排收益，值得运营商开展低成本试点。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning post-training dominates modern language-model development, yet its power behavior on GPU hardware has not been characterized, and datacenters manage GPU power with workload-blind mechanisms, static caps and reactive throttling, that slow hardware indiscriminately. We instrument GRPO training with half-second power telemetry at 7B, 14B, and 72B scales on one to four A100s (380,000+ samples), and train a PPO meta-controller that adapts the workload's own generation parameters to measured power. Against the full 500-step 7B trace, the controller cuts power-limit violations by 89.8% while increasing token output by 18.1% and energy efficiency by 26.2% (tokens per MWh). Deployed live at 72B, the same controller family yields replicated null results, diagnosed as the group-size actuator losing authority under model sharding. An actuator-authority sweep shows the same parameters applied as generation concurrency retain 17-22% power authority, isolating an occupancy-versus-volume principle; a controller rebuilt on that actuator controls a live 72B rollout-generation workload across three replications: 35.7% more output than a static safe baseline at 2.27 +/- 1.08% budget violations, 87.2% fewer violations than uncontrolled operation, and the best mean throughput and energy per token among constrained controllers, with an adaptive threshold rule matching it in one of three operating conditions. Under realistic measurement windows the original 72B transients fall from 23.6% at half-second resolution to 1.6% at 30 s and zero at 5 min; a composed 16-GPU fleet shows zero violations at 30 s and longer, with peak demand at 50-56% of nameplate. For this fleet mix, roughly twofold oversubscription of nameplate appears feasible, subject to operator validation. We quantify the economic and carbon consequences and specify a low-cost operator pilot.

</details>


### [70] [Forecasting Side Effects of Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11227)
*Chong Yong Ong,Alson Wei Jie Sim,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）技术的副作用可预测性问题，通过构建跨效果矩阵，发现副作用是常见、结构化且不对称的，并且可以在引导前就被预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导虽能有效改变语言模型行为且无需重新训练，但常产生意外副作用，影响安全部署。作者希望回答：能否在应用引导之前预测这些副作用？从而支持主动安全审计和更明智的部署决策。

Method: 在三个开源语言模型上，对67种行为构建跨效果矩阵（cross-effect matrix），系统评估引导某一行为时对其他行为产生的副作用。将副作用按量级和方向分别建模，并利用模型的未引导表征来预测副作用方向，与简单基线进行比较。

Result: 副作用普遍存在、结构性强且往往不对称，无法用现有的基于相似性的启发式方法解释。副作用的大小主要取决于目标行为，而副作用的方向可以从模型的未引导表征中以显著高于简单基线的准确率进行预测。

Conclusion: 激活引导具有系统性和可预测的副作用，这为主动安全审计和更明智地部署引导干预提供了可能。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies a language model by adding a learned direction to its hidden activations, enabling targeted behavioral changes without retraining. While effective, steering often produces unintended side effects on other behaviors, making it difficult to deploy safely. We therefore ask: can these side effects be forecasted before steering is applied? We answer this question by constructing a cross-effect matrix over a taxonomy of 67 behaviors across three open-weight language models. We find that side effects are common, structured, and often asymmetric, revealing interactions that cannot be explained by existing similarity-based heuristics. Despite this complexity, we show that side effects are largely predictable before steering is performed. Their magnitude depends primarily on the target behavior, while their direction can be forecasted from the model's unsteered representations with substantially higher accuracy than simple baselines. Our results demonstrate that activation steering has systematic and forecastable side effects, enabling proactive safety auditing and more informed deployment of steering interventions.

</details>


### [71] [Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11229)
*Jack Mirenzi,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视基于偏好的奖励学习，主张将人类教师视为主动的知识持有者而非被动查询回答者，将偏好学习重构为人类-自主系统团队协作问题，并通过模拟实验验证了知情教师设计课程优于学习者主导的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好奖励学习将人类教师视为被动预言机，仅回答学习者生成的查询（如"你更喜欢哪种行为？"），这浪费了教师对目标（奖励函数）的已知知识。当奖励的特征维度增长时，教师设计训练示例的效率优势愈发明显，因此需要一种利用教师知识的新框架。

Method: 将偏好学习重铸为人类-自主团队问题，耦合两个行为模型：教师维护对学习者的模型以设计信息丰富的课程；学习者维护对教师模型的二阶模型（心智理论），通过发出结构化偏好约束（理解陈述）使教师对学习者的模型保持同步。
